base_config[k] = v\\\\n        return base_config\\\\n    return config\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_model_and_checkpoint(config: Dict[str, Any], checkpoint_path: str, device: torch.device):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load SmolVLA policy, optionally wrap with IMM, then load checkpoint weights.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    from train import load_smolvla_policy, wrap_with_imm\\\\n\\\\n    policy = load_smolvla_policy(config, device)\\\\n    imm_enabled = config.get(\\\\\\\"imm\\\\\\\", {}).get(\\\\\\\"enabled\\\\\\\", False)\\\\n\\\\n    if imm_enabled:\\\\n        model = \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extract ACIS importance scores from the model.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns (scores, k) or (None, None) for baseline models.\\\\n    Pass noise/time to ensure deterministic scoring images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state, actions, None, None\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    action_dim = policy.config.action_feature.shape[0]\\\\n    loss = losses[:, :, :action_dim].mean()\\\\n ckpt_path)\\n    state = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)\\n    model.load_state_dict(state[\\\"model_state_dict\\\"], strict=False)\\n\\n    model.eval()\\n    return model, imm_enabled\\n\\n\\ndef compute_importance_scores(\\n    model, batch: Dict[str, torch.Tensor], imm_enabled: bool,\\n    noise: torch.Tensor = None, time: torch.Tensor = None,\\n):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract ACIS importance scores from the model.\\n\\n    Returns (scores, k) or (None, None) for baseline models.\\n    Pass noise/time to ensure deterministic scoring across clean vs perturbed.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not imm_enabled:\\n        return None, None\\n\\n    return model.compute_importance_scores(batch, noise=noise, time=time)\\n\\n\\ndef perturb_images(batch: Dict[str, torch.Tensor], epsilon: float) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Add Gaussian noise to image tensors in batch.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    batch_noisy = {}\\n    for key, val in batch.items():\\n        if isinstance(val, torch.Tensor) and \\\"image\\\" in key.lower() and val.is_floating_point():\\n            batch_noisy[key] = (val + epsilon * torch.randn_like(val)).clamp(0, 1)\\n        else:\\n            batch_noisy[key] = val\\n    return batch_noisy\\n\\n\\ndef baseline_forward(policy, batch: Dict[str, torch.Tensor]):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Baseline forward: flow matching loss without action_is_pad masking.\\n\\n    Bypasses SmolVLAPolicy.forward() which dilutes loss via pad masking.\\n    Uses the same approach as IMMSmolVLAWrapper.forward() for consistency.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from lerobot.utils.constants import OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS, OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK\\n\\n    images, img_masks = policy.prepare_images(batch)\\n    state = policy.prepare_state(batch)\\n    lang_tokens = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS]\\n    lang_masks = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK]\\n    actions = policy.prepare_action(batch)\\n\\n    losses = policy.model.forward(\\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state, actions, None, None\\n    )\\n\\n    action_dim = policy.config.action_feature.shape[0]\\n    loss = losses[:, :, :action_dim].mean()\\n    return loss, {\\\"action_loss\\\": loss.detach()}\\n\\n\\ndef _find_connector(model):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Find the visual connector module in SmolVLA (multi_modal_projector or connector).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for name, module in model.named_modules():\\n        if name.endswith(\\\".connector\\\") or \\\"multi_modal_projector\\\" in name:\\n            return module\\n    return None\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01F5cmXg96cLqM5mRL49ppxr","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Margin-aware token selection: soft masking (train) / hard prune+merge (inference).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\nfrom torch import Tensor\\\\nfrom typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional\\\\n\\\\nfrom .soft_topk import SoftTopK\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass MarginAwareTokenSelector(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(self, temperature: float = 1.0, merge_weight_temp: float = 0.5):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.soft_topk = SoftTopK(temperature=temperature, method=\\\\\\\"sigmoid\\\\\\\")\\\\n        self.merge_weight_temp = merge_weight_temp\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        importance_scores: Tensor,\\\\n        visual_tokens: Tensor,\\\\n        k: int,\\\\n        training: bool = True,\\\\n    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\\\\n        if training:\\\\n            return self._soft_forward(importance_scores, visual_tokens, k)\\\\n        return self._hard_forward(importance_scores, visual_tokens, k)\\\\n\\\\n    def _soft_forward(\\\\n        self, scores: Tensor, tokens: Tensor, k: int\\\\n    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\\\\n        mask, s_k, s_k1 = self.soft_topk(scores, k)\\\\n        selected = tokens * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\\\\n        info = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"mode\\\\\\\": torch.tensor(0),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"mask_sum\\\\\\\": mask.sum(-1).detach(),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"margin\\\\\\\": (s_k - s_k1).detach(),\\\\n        }\\\\n        return selected, mask, info\\\\n\\\\n    def _hard_forward(\\\\n        self, scores: Tensor, tokens: Tensor, k: int\\\\n    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\\\\n        B, N, D = tokens.shape\\\\n\\\\n        sorted_scores, sorted_idx = torch.sort(scores, dim=-1, descending=True)\\\\n        s_k = sorted_scores[:, k - 1]          # (B,) k-th largest score\\\\n        s_k_plus_1 = sorted_scores[:, k]       # (B,) (k+1)-th largest score\\\\n        delta_k = s_k - s_k_plus_1             # (B,) margin\\\\n\\\\n        threshold_high = (s_k + delta_k / 2).unsqueeze(-1)   # (B, 1)\\\\n        threshold_low = (s_k - delta_k / 2).unsqueeze(-1)    # (B, 1)\\\\n\\\\n        preserve_idx = sorted_idx[:, :k]                                 # (B, k)\\\\n        is_topk = torch.zeros(B, N, device=scores.device, dtype=torch.bool)\\\\n        is_topk.scatter_(1, preserve_idx, True)\\\\n\\\\n        borderline_mask = ~is_topk & (scores >= threshold_low) & (scores <= threshold_high)\\\\n        prune_mask = ~is_topk & (scores < threshold_low)\\\\n\\\\n        binary_mask = is_topk.float()\\\\n        selected = tokens * binary_mask.unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, N, D) - same shape as soft\\\\n\\\\n        info = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"mode\\\\\\\": torch.tensor(1),  # 1 = hard\\\\n            \\\\\\\"num_preserved\\\\\\\": is_topk.sum(-1).float().detach(),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"num_pruned\\\\\\\": prune_mask.sum(-1).float().detach(),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"num_merged\\\\\\\": borderline_mask.sum(-1).float().detach(),\\\\n            \\\\\\\"delta_k\\\\\\\": delta_k.detach(),\\\\n        }\\\\n        return selected, binary_mask, info\\\\n\\\\n    def _merge_borderline_tokens(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        tokens: Tensor,\\\\n        scores: Tensor,\\\\n        preserved_tokens: Tensor,\\\\n        preserve_idx: Tensor,\\\\n        borderline_mask: Tensor,\\\\n    ) -> Tensor:\\\\n        B, N, D = tokens.shape\\\\n        K = preserved_tokens.shape[1]\\\\n\\\\n        num_borderline = borderline_mask.sum(-1)\\\\n        if num_borderline.sum() == 0:\\\\n            return preserved_tokens\\\\n\\\\n        preserved_norm = F.normalize(preserved_tokens, dim=-1)\\\\n        tokens_norm = F.normalize(tokens, dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n        sim = \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Margin-aware token selection: soft masking (train) / hard prune+merge (inference).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\nfrom torch import Tensor\\nfrom typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional\\n\\nfrom .soft_topk import SoftTopK\\n\\n\\nclass MarginAwareTokenSelector(nn.Module):\\n    def __init__(self, temperature: float = 1.0, merge_weight_temp: float = 0.5):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.soft_topk = SoftTopK(temperature=temperature, method=\\\"sigmoid\\\")\\n        self.merge_weight_temp = merge_weight_temp\\n\\n    def forward(\\n        self,\\n        importance_scores: Tensor,\\n        visual_tokens: Tensor,\\n        k: int,\\n        training: bool = True,\\n    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\\n        if training:\\n            return self._soft_forward(importance_scores, visual_tokens, k)\\n        return self._hard_forward(importance_scores, visual_tokens, k)\\n\\n    def _soft_forward(\\n        self, scores: Tensor, tokens: Tensor, k: int\\n    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\\n        mask, s_k, s_k1 = self.soft_topk(scores, k)\\n        selected = tokens * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\\n        info = {\\n            \\\"mode\\\": torch.tensor(0),\\n            \\\"mask_sum\\\": mask.sum(-1).detach(),\\n            \\\"margin\\\": (s_k - s_k1).detach(),\\n        }\\n        return selected, mask, info\\n\\n    def _hard_forward(\\n        self, scores: Tensor, tokens: Tensor, k: int\\n    ) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor, Dict[str, Tensor]]:\\n        B, N, D = tokens.shape\\n\\n        sorted_scores, sorted_idx = torch.sort(scores, dim=-1, descending=True)\\n        s_k = sorted_scores[:, k - 1]          # (B,) k-th largest score\\n        s_k_plus_1 = sorted_scores[:, k]       # (B,) (k+1)-th largest score\\n        delta_k = s_k - s_k_plus_1             # (B,) margin\\n\\n        threshold_high = (s_k + delta_k / 2).unsqueeze(-1)   # (B, 1)\\n        threshold_low = (s_k - delta_k / 2).unsqueeze(-1)    # (B, 1)\\n\\n        preserve_idx = sorted_idx[:, :k]                                 # (B, k)\\n        is_topk = torch.zeros(B, N, device=scores.device, dtype=torch.bool)\\n        is_topk.scatter_(1, preserve_idx, True)\\n\\n        borderline_mask = ~is_topk & (scores >= threshold_low) & (scores <= threshold_high)\\n        prune_mask = ~is_topk & (scores < threshold_low)\\n\\n        binary_mask = is_topk.float()\\n        selected = tokens * binary_mask.unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, N, D) - same shape as soft\\n\\n        info = {\\n            \\\"mode\\\": torch.tensor(1),  # 1 = hard\\n            \\\"num_preserved\\\": is_topk.sum(-1).float().detach(),\\n            \\\"num_pruned\\\": prune_mask.sum(-1).float().detach(),\\n            \\\"num_merged\\\": borderline_mask.sum(-1).float().detach(),\\n            \\\"delta_k\\\": delta_k.detach(),\\n        }\\n        return selected, binary_mask, info\\n\\n    def _merge_borderline_tokens(\\n        self,\\n        tokens: Tensor,\\n        scores: Tensor,\\n        preserved_tokens: Tensor,\\n        preserve_idx: Tensor,\\n        borderline_mask: Tensor,\\n    ) -> Tensor:\\n        B, N, D = tokens.shape\\n        K = preserved_tokens.shape[1]\\n\\n        num_borderline = borderline_mask.sum(-1)\\n        if num_borderline.sum() == 0:\\n            return preserved_tokens\\n\\n        preserved_norm = F.normalize(preserved_tokens, dim=-1)\\n        tokens_norm = F.normalize(tokens, dim=-1)\\n\\n        sim = torch.bmm(tokens_norm, preserved_norm.transpose(1, 2))\\n\\n        best_match = sim.argmax(dim=-1)\\n\\n        preserve_scores = torch.gather(scores, 1, preserve_idx)\\n\\n        merged = preserved_tokens.clone()\\n        for b in range(B):\\n            border_idx = borderline_mask[b].nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]\\n            if border_idx.numel() == 0:\\n                continue\\n            for idx in border_idx:\\n                target = best_match[b, idx].item()\\n                w_border = torch.sigmoid(scores[b, idx] / (self.merge_weight_temp + 1e-8))\\n                w_orig = torch.sigmoid(preserve_scores[b, target] / (self.merge_weight_temp + 1e-8))\\n                total_w = w_orig + w_border + 1e-8\\n                merged[b, target] = (w_orig / total_w) * merged[b, target] + (w_border / total_w) * tokens[b, idx]\\n\\n        return merged\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}}}

If we run the eval twice, will we get the same results? Without reseeding the global RNG per condition, the results depend on the global RNG state which is influenced by previous computations. With preloaded batches and no global reseeding, the global RNG state at the start of each condition depends on how many random numbers were consumed in previous conditions. Since conditions are iterated in a fixed order, this IS deterministic across runs (given the same initial seed).\n\nSince the initial seed is set once in main() and batches are preloaded before evaluation starts, each condition consumes RNG in a fixed sequence. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Q3tcRKasK6jeaonMazSRbC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The model loading is slow (takes >30s), which is expected for a VLA model. Creating the SmolVLA policy (downloading/loading model architecture)\n2. Creating the IMM wrapper\n3. Loading checkpoint weights from disk\n\nThis should complete in about 60-90 seconds. So the earlier processes weren't crashing — they were just taking a long time to load, and perhaps I killed them prematurely!\n\nLet me check: the first process (123370) was running for 2.5+ minutes. \n\nGPU 1 never showed any memory usage, and GPU 0 is only showing 2.4GB despite running the training job — that seems surprisingly low for a VLA model. The Blackwell GPU has 97GB of VRAM, so either `nvidia-smi` isn't capturing all the CUDA memory types, or there's some shared memory architecture at play that's not being reported fully.\n\nThe real issue might be that my earlier processes weren't actually crashing — they were just taking a long time to load the model, and I killed them before they finished or the SSH connection timed out. But process 124520 from my nohup died on its own with an empty log, which suggests it might have been a child process that got orphaned when the SSH session closed. With `setsid`, the process should survive the session ending, but maybe the nohup bash wrapper itself dies when SSH closes, leaving the child with broken stdout/stderr pipes.\n\nLooking at the `nohup bash -c` command structure, nohup should protect against SIGHUP and redirect file descriptors, so it ought to survive an SSH disconnect. But my second attempt with `setsid /tmp/run_dryrun.sh` also disappeared after about 2 minutes, which is strange since `setsid` creates a new session that should definitely outlive the parent process.\n\nLet me verify whether that process actually died or if I'm misreading the timing. Rather than keep troubleshooting signal handling, I should just use tmux to run this properly — that'll give me a persistent session that survives
